================================================================================
      PĀṆINIAN SŪTRAS REFERENCE: AṢṬĀDHYĀYĪ THEMATIC COMPILATION
================================================================================

SCOPE OF THIS COMPILATION (EXHAUSTIVE EXPANSION):
  1. SUBANTA (Declension of Base Words / Prātipadikas: Sup Ingestion & Rules)
  2. TIṄANTA (Verbal Derivations, Lakāras, Pada, Tiṅ Endings, Vikaraṇas & Operations)
  3. KṚDANTA (Complete Primary Nominal Derivations: Kṛtya, Pūrva-Kṛdanta, Uttara-Kṛdanta)
  4. PREFIXED TIṄANTA (Upasarga + Verb: Pada Flips, Ṣatva, Ṇatva, Augment Rules)
  5. PREFIXED KṚDANTA (Upasarga + Kṛdanta: Ktvā->Lyap, Affix Conditions, Ṇatva, Lengthening)
  6. NĀMADHĀTUS (Denominative Verbs: Stems, Tiṅanta, Kṛdanta, and Upasargas)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

********************************************************************************
CATEGORY: 1. SUBANTA (DECLENSION FROM A BASE WORD / PRĀTIPADIKA)
********************************************************************************

OVERVIEW:
  This category contains the Pāṇinian sūtras governing the attachment and modification of the 21 nominal case inflections (sup) to a base word (prātipadika). It strictly covers base classification (pada, bha, sarvanāmasthāna), case replacements across masculine, feminine, neuter, and pronominal stems, stem-vowel lengthening/guṇa, and final sandhi operations, excluding kāraka syntax and nominal compounds.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

--- 1.1 Ingestion of Case Endings (Sup) on Prātipadikas ---

[4.1.1] ङ्याप्प्रातिपदिकात् (ṅyāpprātipadikāt)
   Pāṇinian Role: Authority rule (adhikāra): case suffixes (sup) and feminine suffixes are attached after stems ending in ṅī, āp, and prātipadikas.

[4.1.2] स्वौजसमौट्छष्टाभ्याम्भिस्ङेभ्याम्भ्यस्ङसिभ्याम्भ्यस्ङसोसाङ्ङ्योस्सुप् (svaujasamauṭchaṣṭābhyāmbhisṅebhyāmbhyasṅasibhyāmbhyasṅasosāṅṅyossup)
   Pāṇinian Role: Enumerates the 21 nominal case inflections (sup): su, au, jas (Nominative); am, auṭ, śas (Accusative); ṭā, bhyām, bhis (Instrumental); ṅe, bhyām, bhyas (Dative); ṅasi, bhyām, bhyas (Ablative); ṅas, os, ām (Genitive); ṅi, os, sup (Locative).

--- 1.2 Case Designations, Vibhakti, Number & Base Classification ---

[1.2.45] अर्थवदधातुरप्रत्ययः प्रातिपदिकम् (arthavadadhāturapratyayaḥ prātipadikam)
   Pāṇinian Role: Defines 'prātipadika' (nominal base) as any meaningful linguistic form that is neither a verbal root (dhātu) nor a suffix (pratyaya).

[1.2.46] कृत्तद्धितसमासाश्च (kṛttaddhitasamāsāśca)
   Pāṇinian Role: Extends 'prātipadika' designation to forms ending in kṛt (primary) affixes, taddhita (secondary) affixes, and compounds (samāsa).

[1.2.47] ह्रस्वो नपुंसके प्रातिपदिकस्य (hrasvo napuṃsake prātipadikasya)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes shortening (hrasva) of the final vowel of a nominal base in the neuter gender.

[1.2.48] गोस्त्रियोरुपसर्जनस्य (gostriyorupasarjanasya)
   Pāṇinian Role: Shortens the final vowel of 'go' and feminine suffixes (ṅī, āp) when they occur as a subordinate member (upasarjana) in a compound.

[1.4.14] सुप्तिङन्तं पदम् (suptiṅantaṃ padam)
   Pāṇinian Role: Assigns 'pada' designation to any linguistic form ending in a sup or tiṅ suffix.

[1.4.17] स्वादिष्वसर्वनामस्थाने (svādiṣvasarvanāmasthāne)
   Pāṇinian Role: Assigns 'pada' designation to a stem before non-sarvanāmasthāna consonant-initial case endings (bhyām, bhis, bhyas, su).

[1.4.18] यचि भम् (yaci bham)
   Pāṇinian Role: Assigns 'bha' designation to a stem before y-initial or vowel-initial case endings (excluding sarvanāmasthāna).

[1.4.102] तान्येकवचनद्विवचनबहुवचनान्येकशः (tānyekavacanadvivacanabahuvacanānyekaśaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Within each triad, the three suffixes are designated singular (ekavacana), dual (dvivacana), and plural (bahuvacana).

[1.4.103] सुपः (supaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Designates the 21 nominal inflections as 'sup'.

[1.4.104] विभक्तिश्च (vibhaktiśca)
   Pāṇinian Role: Designates 'sup' and 'tiṅ' triads as 'vibhakti' (cases/inflections).

[1.1.23] बहुगणवतुडति संख्या (bahugaṇavatuḍati saṃkhyā)
   Pāṇinian Role: Assigns the technical designation 'saṅkhyā' (numeral) to bahu, gaṇa, and words ending in suffixes vatu and ḍati.

[1.1.24] ष्णान्ता षट् (ṣṇāntā ṣaṭ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Assigns the designation 'ṣaṭ' to numeral stems ending in the consonants ṣ or n (e.g. pañcan, ṣaṣ, saptan).

[1.1.25] डति च (ḍati ca)
   Pāṇinian Role: Assigns the designation 'ṣaṭ' to numerals ending in the suffix ḍati (e.g. kati).

[1.1.27] सर्वादीनि सर्वनामानि (sarvādīni sarvanāmāni)
   Pāṇinian Role: Assigns the technical designation 'sarvanāma' (pronoun) to words in the traditional list beginning with 'sarva'.

[1.1.28] विभाषा दिक्समासे बहुव्रीहौ (vibhāṣā diksamāse bahuvrīhau)
   Pāṇinian Role: Sarvanāma designation is optional in a bahuvrīhi compound denoting an intermediate direction (e.g. uttarapūrvāyai / uttarapūrvāyai).

[1.1.29] न बहुव्रीहौ (na bahuvrīhau)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prohibits sarvanāma designation in other bahuvrīhi compounds (e.g. priyaviśvāya).

[1.1.30] तृतीयासमासे (tṛtīyāsamāse)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prohibits sarvanāma designation in instrumental tatpuruṣa compounds (e.g. māsapūrvāya).

[1.1.31] द्वन्द्वे च (dvandve ca)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prohibits sarvanāma designation for the first member in a dvandva compound before case endings (e.g. varṇāśramebhyaḥ).

[1.1.32] विभाषा जसि (vibhāṣā jasi)
   Pāṇinian Role: Pronoun designation is optional in dvandva compounds before the nominative plural case ending 'jas' (e.g. sarvaviśve / sarvaviśvāḥ).

[1.1.33] प्रथमचरमतयाल्पार्धकतिपयनेमाश्च (prathamacaramatayālpārdhakatipayanemāśca)
   Pāṇinian Role: Optional sarvanāma designation before nominative plural 'jas' for prathama, carama, suffixes ending in taya, alpa, ardha, katipaya, and nema.

[1.1.34] पूर्वपरावरदक्षिणोत्तरापराधराणि व्यवस्थायामसंज्ञायाम् (pūrvaparāvaradakṣiṇottarāparādharāṇi vyavasthāyāmasaṃjñāyām)
   Pāṇinian Role: Words pūrva, para, avara, dakṣiṇa, uttara, apara, and adhara are optionally sarvanāma before jas in relative positioning when not proper names.

[1.1.35] स्वमज्ञातिधनाख्यायाम् (svamajñātidhanākhyāyām)
   Pāṇinian Role: The word 'sva' is optionally sarvanāma before nominative plural 'jas' when not meaning kinsman (jñāti) or property (dhana).

[1.1.36] अन्तरं बहिर्योगोपसंव्यानयोः (antaraṃ bahiryogopasaṃvyānayoḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: The word 'antara' is optionally sarvanāma before nominative plural 'jas' when meaning exterior (bahiryoga) or undergarment (upasaṃvyāna).

[1.1.42] शि सर्वनामस्थानम् (śi sarvanāmasthānam)
   Pāṇinian Role: Designates the neuter plural suffix 'śi' (replacing jas and śas) as sarvanāmasthāna.

[1.1.43] सुडनपुंसकस्य (suḍanapuṃsakasya)
   Pāṇinian Role: Designates the first five case inflections (su, au, jas, am, auṭ) of non-neuter stems as sarvanāmasthāna.

[7.1.39] सुपां सुलुक्पूर्वसवर्णाऽऽच्छेयाडाड्यायाजालः (supāṃ sulukpūrvasavarṇā''ccheyāḍāḍyāyājālaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Vedic archaism rule: case suffixes (sup) undergo diverse elisions (luk), substitutions (śe, yā, ḍā, ḍyā, yāc, āl), or lengthening.

[7.1.50] आज्जसेरसुक् (ājjaserasuk)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes the augment asuk before the nominative plural ending jas after stems ending in ā (Vedic usage).

--- 1.4 Case Augments (Āgamas in 7.1: suṭ, nuṭ, num, anaṅ) ---

[7.1.52] आमि सर्वनाम्नः सुट् (āmi sarvanāmnaḥ suṭ)

[7.1.53] त्रेस्त्रयः (trestrayaḥ)

--- 1.5 Stem Inflexion & Irregular Prātipadika Replacements (7.1 & 7.2) ---

--- 1.8 Vowel Sandhi Rules Specific to Sup (6.1) ---

[6.1.68] हल्ङ्याब्भ्यो दीर्घात् सुतिस्यपृक्तं हल् (halṅyābbhyo dīrghāt sutisyapṛktaṃ hal)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes elision of single-consonant case/tiṅ endings (apṛkta lopa) or vocative shortening (halṅyābbhyo dīrghāt sutisyapṛktaṃ hal).

[6.1.102] प्रथमयोः पूर्वसवर्णः (prathamayoḥ pūrvasavarṇaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Governs single-substitute vowel sandhi (pūrva-savarṇa, ami pūrva) between nominal base and case endings (prathamayoḥ pūrvasavarṇaḥ).

--- 1.9 Pada-Anta Sandhi & Consonant Transformations (8.2 & 8.3) ---

[8.2.7] नलोपः प्रातिपदिकान्तस्य (nalopaḥ prātipadikāntasya)

   Pāṇinian Role: Governs word-final s/j to ru transformation (sasajuṣo ruḥ) in nominal and verbal padas (sipi dhāto rurvā).

[8.2.75] दश्च (daśca)
   Pāṇinian Role: Governs word-final s/j to ru transformation (sasajuṣo ruḥ) in nominal and verbal padas (daśca).

[8.3.15] खरवसानयोर्विसर्जनीयः (kharavasānayorvisarjanīyaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Replaces pada-final 'r' (ru) with visarjanīya (visarga ḥ) before unvoiced consonants (khar) or in sentence pause (avasāna).

[8.3.17] भोभगोअघोअपूर्वस्य योऽशि (bhobhagoaghoapūrvasya yo'śi)
   Pāṇinian Role: Replaces ru with y before voiced sounds (aś) when preceded by bhoḥ, bhagoḥ, aghoḥ, or short/long a.

[8.3.22] हलि सर्वेषाम् (hali sarveṣām)
   Pāṇinian Role: Elides the semivowel y resulting from ru before any consonant (hal).

[8.3.57] इण्कोः (iṇkoḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Adhikāra condition for retroflexion (ṣatva): occurs after letters of the iṇ pratyāhāra (all vowels except a/ā, plus y, v, r, l) or the velar class (ku).

[8.3.59] आदेशप्रत्यययोः (ādeśapratyayayoḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes retroflexion of dental s -> retroflex ṣ when it is an ādeśa (substitute) or a pratyaya (suffix) preceded by iṇ or ku (e.g. rāmeṣu, hariṣu).

[8.3.107] सुञः (suñaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Condition for verbal ṣatva (dental s -> retroflex ṣ) after prefixes/augments (suñaḥ).


********************************************************************************
CATEGORY: 2. TIṄANTA (VERBAL DERIVATIONS & CONJUGATION)
********************************************************************************

OVERVIEW:
  This category contains the Pāṇinian sūtras governing verbal inflection: lakārotpatti (tenses/aspects), voice (prayoga), puruṣa and vacana allocation, the 18 tiṅ personal endings, gaṇa vikaraṇas (śap, śyan, etc.), aorist markers (cli/sic), personal ending replacements, ārdhadhātuka iṭ augment, abhyāsa/reduplication, aṭ/āṭ past augments, guṇa/vṛddhi, and verbal stem consonant transformations.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

--- 2.1 Lakārotpatti (Tense, Mood & Aspect Designations: 3.2 & 3.3) ---

--- 2.2 Voice, Puruṣa & Vacana Allocation (1.3, 1.4 & 3.4) ---

[1.3.12] अनुदात्तङित आत्मनेपदम् (anudāttaṅita ātmanepadam)
   Pāṇinian Role: Verbal roots with an anudātta or ṅ as an it-marker take Ātmanepada personal endings in kartari prayoga (e.g. edhate, vartate).

[1.3.13] भावकर्मणोः (bhāvakarmaṇoḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: All verbal roots take Ātmanepada endings in impersonal (bhāve) and passive (karmaṇi) voice.

[1.3.14] कर्तरि कर्मव्यतिहारे (kartari karmavyatihāre)
   Pāṇinian Role: Roots take Ātmanepada endings when reciprocity of action (karmavyatihāra) is expressed (e.g. vyatiluṇanti -> vyatilunate).

[1.3.15] न गतिहिंसार्थेभ्यः (na gatihiṃsārthebhyaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes Ātmanepada endings for roots yam and han when denoting dissimulation or concealing the truth (apahnava).

[1.3.16] इतरेतरान्योन्योपपदाच्च (itaretarānyonyopapadācca)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes Ātmanepada endings when accompanied by the reciprocal upapadas 'itaretara' or 'anyonya'.

[1.3.78] शेषात् कर्तरि परस्मैपदम् (śeṣāt kartari parasmaipadam)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes Parasmaipada endings for the root when preceded by the specified upasarga or grammatical condition (śeṣāt kartari parasmaipadam).

[1.4.99] लः परस्मैपदम् (laḥ parasmaipadam)
   Pāṇinian Role: Personal verbal endings (tiṅ) replacing lakāras are designated 'parasmaipada'.

[1.4.100] तङानावात्मनेपदम् (taṅānāvātmanepadam)
   Pāṇinian Role: The nine taṅ endings (ta, ātām, jha, thās, āthām, dhvam, iṭ, vahi, mahiṅ) and the kṛt participles śānac and kānac (āna) are designated 'ātmanepada'.

[1.4.101] तिङस्त्रीणि त्रीणि प्रथममध्यमोत्तमाः (tiṅastrīṇi trīṇi prathamamadhyamottamāḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: The 18 tiṅ suffixes are divided into triads designated as prathama (third person), madhyama (second person), and uttama (first person).

[1.4.102] तान्येकवचनद्विवचनबहुवचनान्येकशः (tānyekavacanadvivacanabahuvacanānyekaśaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Within each triad, the three suffixes are designated singular (ekavacana), dual (dvivacana), and plural (bahuvacana).

[1.4.103] सुपः (supaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Designates the 21 nominal inflections as 'sup'.

[1.4.104] विभक्तिश्च (vibhaktiśca)
   Pāṇinian Role: Designates 'sup' and 'tiṅ' triads as 'vibhakti' (cases/inflections).

[1.4.105] युष्मद्युपपदे समानाधिकरणे स्थानिन्यपि मध्यमः (yuṣmadyupapade samānādhikaraṇe sthāninyapi madhyamaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Assigns 'madhyama puruṣa' (second person) when accompanying or referring to 'yuṣmad' (you).

[1.4.106] प्रहासे च मन्योपपदे मन्यतेरुत्तम एकवच्च (prahāse ca manyopapade manyateruttama ekavacca)
   Pāṇinian Role: Assigns the designation 'madhyama puruṣa' (second person) when accompanying or referring to 'yuṣmad' (you).

[1.4.107] अस्मद्युत्तमः (asmadyuttamaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Assigns 'uttama puruṣa' (first person) when accompanying or referring to 'asmad' (I/we).

[1.4.108] शेषे प्रथमः (śeṣe prathamaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Assigns 'prathama puruṣa' (third person) in all other cases (śeṣe prathamaḥ).

[3.4.69] लः कर्मणि च भावे चाकर्मकेभ्यः (laḥ karmaṇi ca bhāve cākarmakebhyaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes gerundial, infinitival, or participial suffix (ktvā, tumun, ṇamul, kta, ktavatu) after roots (laḥ karmaṇi ca bhāve cākarmakebhyaḥ).

[1.3.36] सम्माननोत्सञ्जनाचार्यकरणज्ञानभृतिविगणनव्ययेषु नियः (sammānanotsañjanācāryakaraṇajñānabhṛtivigaṇanavyayeṣu niyaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes Ātmanepada endings for the root when preceded by the specified upasarga or attendant semantic condition (sammānanotsañjanācāryakaraṇajñānabhṛtivigaṇanavyayeṣu niyaḥ).

--- 2.4 Vikaraṇas & Gaṇa Affixes (3.1 & 2.4) ---

[3.1.68] कर्तरि शप् (kartari śap)
   Pāṇinian Role: Inserts the class 1 (bhvādi) vikaraṇa 'śap' (surface 'a') between the verbal stem and a sārvadhātuka suffix in active voice (kartari).

[3.1.69] दिवादिभ्यः श्यन् (divādibhyaḥ śyan)
   Pāṇinian Role: Inserts the class 4 (divādi) vikaraṇa 'śyan' (surface 'ya') between root and sārvadhātuka suffix in active voice (e.g. dīvyati).

[3.1.70] वा भ्राशभ्लाशभ्रमुक्रमुक्लमुत्रसित्रुटिलषः (vā bhrāśabhlāśabhramukramuklamutrasitruṭilaṣaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes optional śyan (divādi vikaraṇa) for roots bhrāś, bhlāś, bhram, kram, klam, tras, truṭ, and laṣ.

[3.1.71] यसोऽनुपसर्गात् (yaso'nupasargāt)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes the vikaraṇa śyan for root yas when not preceded by an upasarga.

[3.1.72] संयसश्च (saṃyasaśca)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes optional śyan for root yas when preceded by the prefix saṃ.

[3.1.73] स्वादिभ्यः श्नुः (svādibhyaḥ śnuḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Inserts the class 5 (svādi) vikaraṇa 'śnu' (surface 'nu') between root and sārvadhātuka suffix in active voice (e.g. sunoti).

[3.1.74] श्रुवः शृ च (śruvaḥ śṛ ca)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes the vikaraṇa śa (tudādi) for roots muc, lup, vyadh, vyac, vraśc, pṛcch, bhrajj, and roots ending in cha.

[3.1.75] अक्षोऽन्यतरस्याम् (akṣo'nyatarasyām)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes optional śa vikaraṇa for root akṣ.

[3.1.76] तनूकरणे तक्षः (tanūkaraṇe takṣaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes optional śa vikaraṇa for roots krand and śam.

[3.1.77] तुदादिभ्यः शः (tudādibhyaḥ śaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Inserts the class 6 (tudādi) vikaraṇa 'śa' (surface 'a', unaccented, without guṇa) between root and sārvadhātuka suffix (e.g. tudati).

[3.1.78] रुधादिभ्यः श्नम् (rudhādibhyaḥ śnam)
   Pāṇinian Role: Inserts the class 7 (rudhādi) vikaraṇa 'śnam' (surface 'na/n' as an infixed nasal) after the last vowel of the root (e.g. ruṇaddhi).

[3.1.79] तनादिकृञ्भ्य उः (tanādikṛñbhya uḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Inserts the class 8 (tanādi) vikaraṇa 'u' after tanādi roots and kṛñ before sārvadhātuka suffixes (e.g. tanoti, karoti).

[3.1.80] धिन्विकृण्व्योर च (dhinvikṛṇvyora ca)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes vikaraṇa u with final root replacement 'a' for dhinv and kṛṇv.

[3.1.81] क्र्यादिभ्यः श्ना (kryādibhyaḥ śnā)
   Pāṇinian Role: Inserts the class 9 (kryādi) vikaraṇa 'śnā' (surface 'nā/nī') after kryādi roots before sārvadhātuka suffixes (e.g. krīṇāti).

[3.1.82] स्तन्भुस्तुन्भुस्कन्भुस्कुन्भुस्कुञ्भ्यः श्नुश्च (stanbhustunbhuskanbhuskunbhuskuñbhyaḥ śnuśca)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes vikaraṇa śnu (in addition to śnā) for roots stambh, stumbh, skambh, skumbh, and skubh.

[3.1.83] हलः श्नः शानज्झौ (halaḥ śnaḥ śānajjhau)
   Pāṇinian Role: Replaces the vikaraṇa śnā with śānac before personal ending hi in imperative (loṭ).

[3.1.84] छन्दसि शायजपि (chandasi śāyajapi)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes diverse vikaraṇas in Vedic usage (bahulaṃ chandasi).

[3.1.85] व्यत्ययो बहुलम् (vyatyayo bahulam)
   Pāṇinian Role: Governs transposition (vyatyaya) of voice, person, number, and vikaraṇa in Vedic usage.

[3.1.86] लिङ्याशिष्यङ् (liṅyāśiṣyaṅ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes aṅ (cli substitute) for roots in puṣādi, as, and dyut in parasmaipada.

[3.1.67] सार्वधातुके यक् (sārvadhātuke yak)
   Pāṇinian Role: Inserts the vikaraṇa 'yak' (surface 'ya') after verbal roots before sārvadhātuka suffixes in passive and impersonal voice (bhāvakarmaṇoḥ, e.g. kriyate, bhūyate).

[3.1.33] स्यतासी लृलुटोः (syatāsī lṛluṭoḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes the tense-stem suffixes 'sya' for simple future / conditional (lṛṭ / lṛṅ) and 'tāsī' for periphrastic future (luṭ).

[3.1.34] सिब्बहुलं लेटि (sibbahulaṃ leṭi)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes the augment sip optionally in the Vedic subjunctive (leṭ).

[3.1.35] कास्प्रत्ययादाममन्त्रे लिटि (kāspratyayādāmamantre liṭi)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes the secondary suffix 'ām' in the perfect tense (Liṭ) after roots beginning with kās or ending in a pratyaya (including denominatives: putrīyāñcakāra).

[3.1.36] इजादेश्च गुरुमतोऽनृच्छः (ijādeśca gurumato'nṛcchaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes ām-pratyaya in Liṭ for verbal roots beginning with a vowel (ic) and heavy (guru).

[3.1.37] दयायासश्च (dayāyāsaśca)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes ām-pratyaya in Liṭ for roots day, aya, and ās.

[3.1.38] उषविदजागृभ्योऽन्यतरस्याम् (uṣavidajāgṛbhyo'nyatarasyām)
   Pāṇinian Role: Optional ām-pratyaya in Liṭ for roots uṣ, vid, and jāgṛ.

[3.1.39] भीह्रीभृहुवां श्लुवच्च (bhīhrībhṛhuvāṃ śluvacca)
   Pāṇinian Role: Optional ām-pratyaya in Liṭ for roots bhī and hrī.

[3.1.40] कृञ् चानुप्रयुज्यते लिटि (kṛñ cānuprayujyate liṭi)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes that the auxiliary roots kṛ, bhū, or as must be appended after a stem ending in 'ām' in Liṭ (e.g. īkṣāñcakre, putrīyāñcakāra).

[3.1.41] विदाङ्कुर्वन्त्वित्यन्यतरस्याम् (vidāṅkurvantvityanyatarasyām)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes irregular imperative periphrastic formation with vidāṅkurvantu.

[3.1.42] अभ्युत्सादयांप्रजनयांचिकयांरमयामकः पावयांक्रियाद्विदामक्रन्निति च्छन्दसि (abhyutsādayāṃprajanayāṃcikayāṃramayāmakaḥ pāvayāṃkriyādvidāmakranniti cchandasi)
   Pāṇinian Role: Word-order rule governing the precedence of auxiliary verbs (kṛ, bhū, as) in periphrastic formations.

[3.1.43] च्लि लुङि (cli luṅi)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes the general aorist marker 'cli' after verbal roots before personal endings in the aorist tense (Luṅ).

[3.1.44] च्लेः सिच् (cleḥ sic)
   Pāṇinian Role: General rule: replaces the aorist marker 'cli' with 'sic' (surface 's') in Luṅ.

[3.1.45] शल इगुपधादनिटः क्सः (śala igupadhādaniṭaḥ ksaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Replaces 'cli' with 'ksa' (surface 'sa') in aniṭ roots ending in śal consonants with penultimate ik vowels (e.g. adukṣat).

[3.1.46] श्लिष आलिङ्गने (śliṣa āliṅgane)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes the aorist marker aṅ (cli substitute) for root śliṣ when meaning embracing.

[3.1.47] न दृशः (na dṛśaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prohibits aorist marker aṅ for roots druh, śrudh, snuh, and ndruh.

[3.1.48] णिश्रिद्रुस्रुभ्यः कर्तरि चङ् (ṇiśridrusrubhyaḥ kartari caṅ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Replaces 'cli' with 'caṅ' (reduplicated aorist) in causative (ṇi) stems and roots śri, dru, sru in active voice (e.g. acīkarat).

[3.1.49] विभाषा धेट्श्व्योः (vibhāṣā dheṭśvyoḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes aorist cli substitution rules in Vedic (vibhrāṭ-svaritayoḥ).

[3.1.50] गुपेश्छन्दसि (gupeśchandasi)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes aorist marker aṅ for root kliś in intransitive usage.

[3.1.51] नोनयतिध्वनयत्येलयत्यर्दयतिभ्यः (nonayatidhvanayatyelayatyardayatibhyaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes aorist marker aṅ for root as.

[3.1.52] अस्यतिवक्तिख्यातिभ्योऽङ् (asyativaktikhyātibhyo'ṅ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Replaces 'cli' with 'aṅ' after roots as, vac, and khyā in parasmaipada (e.g. āsthat, avocat, akhyat).

[3.1.53] लिपिसिचिह्वश्च (lipisicihvaśca)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes aorist marker aṅ for roots lip, sic, and hve.

[3.1.54] आत्मनेपदेष्वन्यतरस्याम् (ātmanepadeṣvanyatarasyām)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes optional aorist marker aṅ in ātmanepada for roots lip, sic, and hve.

[3.1.55] पुषादिद्युताद्यॢदितः परस्मैपदेषु (puṣādidyutādyḷditaḥ parasmaipadeṣu)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes aorist marker aṅ for the puṣpa class of roots.

[3.1.56] सर्त्तिशास्त्यर्तिभ्यश्च (sarttiśāstyartibhyaśca)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes aorist marker aṅ for roots sṛ and dṛ.

[3.1.57] इरितो वा (irito vā)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes optional aorist marker aṅ for root rādh.

[3.1.58] जॄस्तम्भुम्रुचुम्लुचुग्रुचुग्लुचुग्लुञ्चुश्विभ्यश्च (jṝstambhumrucumlucugrucuglucugluñcuśvibhyaśca)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes optional aorist marker aṅ for root jṛ.

[3.1.59] कृमृदृरुहिभ्यश्छन्दसि (kṛmṛdṛruhibhyaśchandasi)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes optional aorist marker aṅ for root kṣam.

[3.1.60] चिण् ते पदः (ciṇ te padaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes root vowel replacement in root śās before consonant-initial aṅ.

[3.1.61] दीपजनबुधपूरितायिप्यायिभ्योऽन्यतरस्याम् (dīpajanabudhapūritāyipyāyibhyo'nyatarasyām)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes aorist marker caṅ (reduplicated aorist) for roots dīp and dan.

[3.1.62] अचः कर्मकर्तरि (acaḥ karmakartari)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes aorist substitute ciṇ before vowel-initial suffixes in certain roots.

[3.1.63] दुहश्च (duhaśca)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes aorist marker ciṇ for all verbal roots in passive/impersonal voice (bhāve/karmaṇi).

[3.1.64] न रुधः (na rudhaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes ciṇ augment in specified verbal roots.

[3.1.65] तपोऽनुतापे च (tapo'nutāpe ca)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes ciṇ replacement in root raṇ.

[3.1.66] चिण् भावकर्मणोः (ciṇ bhāvakarmaṇoḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes total elision (luk) of the personal ending ta after the passive aorist marker ciṇ (e.g. akāri + ta -> akāri).

[2.4.72] अदिप्रभृतिभ्यः शपः (adiprabhṛtibhyaḥ śapaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes elision (luk) of the vikaraṇa 'śap' after class 2 (adādi) verbal roots (e.g. atti, dvegḍhi).

[2.4.73] बहुलं छन्दसि (bahulaṃ chandasi)
   Pāṇinian Role: Vedic deletion (luk) of the vikaraṇa śap (bahulaṃ chandasi).

[2.4.74] यङोऽचि च (yaṅo'ci ca)
   Pāṇinian Role: Elision rule for vikaraṇa in Vedic contexts.

[2.4.75] जुहोत्यादिभ्यः श्लुः (juhotyādibhyaḥ śluḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes reduplication and elision (ślu) of the vikaraṇa 'śap' after class 3 (juhotyādi) roots (e.g. juhoti, bibheti).

[2.4.76] बहुलं छन्दसि (bahulaṃ chandasi)
   Pāṇinian Role: Vedic reduplicative elision (ślu) in non-juhotyādi roots.

[2.4.77] गातिस्थाघुपाभूभ्यः सिचः परस्मैपदेषु (gātisthāghupābhūbhyaḥ sicaḥ parasmaipadeṣu)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes elision (luk) of the aorist marker 'sic' after gā, sthā, dā, dhā, pā, and bhū in active voice (e.g. agāt, asthāt, abhūt).

[2.4.80] मन्त्रे घसह्वरणशवृदहाद्वृच्कृगमिजनिभ्यो लेः (mantre ghasahvaraṇaśavṛdahādvṛckṛgamijanibhyo leḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes elision (luk) of personal endings after ām in the periphrastic perfect (e.g. īkṣāñcakre).

[2.4.81] आमः (āmaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes elision of personal endings after ām.

[2.4.82] अव्ययादाप्सुपः (avyayādāpsupaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes elision (luk) of sic after certain verbal stems in Luṅ aorist.

[2.4.83] नाव्ययीभावादतोऽम्त्वपञ्चम्याः (nāvyayībhāvādato'mtvapañcamyāḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes elision of sic after root as.

[2.4.84] तृतीयासप्तम्योर्बहुलम् (tṛtīyāsaptamyorbahulam)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes elision (luk) of sic after root bhū in the aorist (e.g. a-bhū-t).

[2.4.85] लुटः प्रथमस्य डारौरसः (luṭaḥ prathamasya ḍāraurasaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Elision rule in aorist for root vibhu in Vedic.

--- 2.6 Tiṅ Replacements & Personal Ending Modifications (7.1) ---

[7.1.3] झोऽन्तः (jho'ntaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes personal ending replacement (tiṅ-ādeśa) for verbal stems (jho'ntaḥ).

[7.1.4] अदभ्यस्तात् (adabhyastāt)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes personal ending replacement (tiṅ-ādeśa) for verbal stems (adabhyastāt).

[7.1.5] आत्मनेपदेष्वनतः (ātmanepadeṣvanataḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes personal ending replacement (tiṅ-ādeśa) for verbal stems (ātmanepadeṣvanataḥ).

[7.1.6] शीङो रुट् (śīṅo ruṭ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes personal ending replacement (tiṅ-ādeśa) for verbal stems (śīṅo ruṭ).

[7.1.7] वेत्तेर्विभाषा (vettervibhāṣā)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes personal ending replacement (tiṅ-ādeśa) for verbal stems (vettervibhāṣā).

[7.1.8] बहुलं छन्दसि (bahulaṃ chandasi)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes personal ending replacement (tiṅ-ādeśa) for verbal stems (bahulaṃ chandasi).

[7.1.34] आत औ णलः (āta au ṇalaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Replaces the 1st and 3rd person singular perfect personal ending ṇal with 'au' after roots ending in ā (e.g. papau, tasthau).

[7.1.35] तुह्योस्तातङ्ङाशिष्यन्यतरस्याम् (tuhyostātaṅṅāśiṣyanyatarasyām)
   Pāṇinian Role: Optional substitution of 'tātaṅ' for 'tu' and 'hi' in benedictive and imperative moods (e.g. bhavatāt).

[7.1.40] अमो मश् (amo maś)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes tiṅ personal ending modification or elision in verbal conjugation (amo maś).

********************************************************************************
CATEGORY: 3. KṚDANTA (PRIMARY NOMINAL DERIVATIONS)
********************************************************************************

OVERVIEW:
  This category contains the entire exhaustive Kṛt adhikāra (Pāṇini 3.1.91 through 3.4.76). It encompasses all primary nominal derivations formed directly from verbal roots: kṛtyas (gerundives of obligation/fitness), pūrva-kṛdanta agent/instrument/locus nouns, uttara-kṛdanta action nouns (bhāve ghañ/lyuṭ/ktin), case nouns (akartari ca kārake), infinitives (tumun), gerunds (ktvā/ṇamul), and participles (kta, ktavatu, śatṛ, śānac, kvasu).

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

--- 3.1 The Kṛt Adhikāra & Kṛtya Affixes (Obligation / Fitness: 3.1.91 - 3.1.132) ---

[3.1.91] धातोः (dhātoḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes kṛtya gerundive suffix (obligation/fitness) after verbal roots under specified conditions (dhātoḥ).

[3.1.92] तत्रोपपदं सप्तमीस्थम् (tatropapadaṃ saptamīstham)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes kṛtya gerundive suffix (obligation/fitness) after verbal roots under specified conditions (tatropapadaṃ saptamīstham).

--- 3.4 Gerunds, Infinitives & Participles (3.4.1 - 3.4.76) ---

   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes gerundial, infinitival, or participial suffix (ktvā, tumun, ṇamul, kta, ktavatu) after roots (kriyāsamabhihāre loṭ; loṭo hisvau; vā ca tadhvamoḥ).

[3.4.74] भीमादयोऽपादाने (bhīmādayo'pādāne)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes gerundial, infinitival, or participial suffix (ktvā, tumun, ṇamul, kta, ktavatu) after roots (bhīmādayo'pādāne).

[3.4.75] ताभ्यामन्यत्रोणादयः (tābhyāmanyatroṇādayaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes gerundial, infinitival, or participial suffix (ktvā, tumun, ṇamul, kta, ktavatu) after roots (tābhyāmanyatroṇādayaḥ).

[3.4.76] क्तोऽधिकरणे च ध्रौव्यगतिप्रत्यवसानार्थेभ्यः (kto'dhikaraṇe ca dhrauvyagatipratyavasānārthebhyaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes gerundial, infinitival, or participial suffix (ktvā, tumun, ṇamul, kta, ktavatu) after roots (kto'dhikaraṇe ca dhrauvyagatipratyavasānārthebhyaḥ).


********************************************************************************
CATEGORY: 4. PREFIXED TIṄANTA (UPASARGA + TIṄANTA)
********************************************************************************

OVERVIEW:
  This category encompasses all Pāṇinian rules governing the interaction of verbal prefixes (upasargas / gati) with conjugated verbs (tiṅanta). It includes prefix syntax and ordering, voice (pada) flips triggered by specific upasargas (1.3), verbal retroflexion of dental s to retroflex ṣ (ṣatva: 8.3.65–8.3.119), verbal retroflexion of dental n to retroflex ṇ (ṇatva: 8.4.14–8.4.39), and prefix-induced suṭ augment / sandhi.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

--- 4.1 Upasarga & Gati Definitions & Word Order (1.4) ---

[1.4.58] प्रादयः (prādayaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Enumerates the 22 prefixes beginning with 'pra' (pra, parā, apa, sam, anu, ava, nis, nir, dus, dur, vi, āṅ, ni, adhi, api, ati, su, ud, abhi, prati, pari, upa).

[1.4.59] उपसर्गाः क्रियायोगे (upasargāḥ kriyāyoge)
   Pāṇinian Role: Designates the 22 prādi particles as 'upasarga' when connected with a verbal action (kriyāyoga).

[1.4.60] गतिश्च (gatiśca)
   Pāṇinian Role: Designates the 22 prādi particles as 'gati' when joined with a verbal action.

[1.4.80] ते प्राग्धातोः (te prāgdhātoḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes that upasargas and gati words are placed immediately before the verbal root in classical Sanskrit (te prāg dhātoḥ).

[1.4.81] छन्दसि परेऽपि (chandasi pare'pi)
   Pāṇinian Role: In Vedic Sanskrit, upasargas may appear after the verb (chandasi pare'pi).

[1.4.82] व्यवहिताश्च (vyavahitāśca)
   Pāṇinian Role: In Vedic Sanskrit, upasargas may be separated from the verb by intervening words (vyavahitāśca).

--- 4.2 Upasarga-Conditioned Voice Flips (1.3 Pada Alterations) ---

   Pāṇinian Role: Specific verbal root condition or scope for ṇatva after prefixes (kṣubhnā''diṣu ca).

--- 4.5 Prefix Sandhi & Suṭ Augment (6.1 & 6.4) ---

[6.1.135] सुट् कात् पूर्वः (suṭ kāt pūrvaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes the augment suṭ before roots when preceded by specific prefixes (suṭ kāt pūrvaḥ).

[6.1.136] अडभ्यासव्यवायेऽपि (aḍabhyāsavyavāye'pi)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes the augment suṭ before roots when preceded by specific prefixes (aḍabhyāsavyavāye'pi).

[6.1.137] सम्पर्युपेभ्यः करोतौ भूषणे (samparyupebhyaḥ karotau bhūṣaṇe)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes the augment suṭ before roots when preceded by specific prefixes (samparyupebhyaḥ karotau bhūṣaṇe).

[6.1.138] समवाये च (samavāye ca)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes the augment suṭ before roots when preceded by specific prefixes (samavāye ca).

[6.1.139] उपात् प्रतियत्नवैकृतवाक्याध्याहारेषु (upāt pratiyatnavaikṛtavākyādhyāhāreṣu)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes the augment suṭ before roots when preceded by specific prefixes (upāt pratiyatnavaikṛtavākyādhyāhāreṣu).


********************************************************************************
CATEGORY: 5. PREFIXED KṚDANTA (PREFIXES + KṚDANTA)
********************************************************************************

OVERVIEW:
  This category compiles the Pāṇinian sūtras governing the morphological and phonological effects of upasargas on primary nominal derivations (kṛdanta). Key operations include the mandatory substitution of ktvā by lyap when preceded by an upasarga (7.1.37), the insertion of the tuk augment (6.1.71), stem lengthening/reduction under lyap, prefix-conditioned kṛt affix selections (e.g. 3.3.92 upasarge ghoḥ kiḥ), retroflexion in kṛtyas (8.4.29 kṛtyacaḥ), and prefix vowel lengthening before kṛt affixes.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

--- 5.1 Ktvā -> Lyap Substitution & Lyap-Conditioned Operations (7.1, 6.1, 6.4) ---

[7.1.37] समासेऽनञ्पूर्वे क्त्वो ल्यप् (samāse'nañpūrve ktvo lyap)
   Pāṇinian Role: In a compound not beginning with the negative particle nañ, the absolutive suffix 'ktvā' is obligatorily replaced by 'lyap' (surface 'ya') when preceded by an upasarga or gati (e.g. pra-kṛ-tya, vi-ji-tya).

[7.1.38] क्त्वाऽपि छन्दसि (ktvā'pi chandasi)
   Pāṇinian Role: Permits the substitution of lyap for ktvā in Vedic usage even without a preceding prefix.

[6.1.71] ह्रस्वस्य पिति कृति तुक् (hrasvasya piti kṛti tuk)
   Pāṇinian Role: Inserts the augment 'tuk' (surface 't') after a short vowel root before a pit kṛt suffix (such as lyap: pra-kṛ-t-ya, saṃ-stu-t-ya).

[6.4.38] वा ल्यपि (vā lyapi)
   Pāṇinian Role: Optional lengthening/elision of root-final consonant in jan, san, khan before lyap (e.g. pra-tām-ya / pra-tam-ya).

[6.4.56] ल्यपि लघुपूर्वात् (lyapi laghupūrvāt)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes deletion of causative 'ṇic' before lyap when preceded by a light syllable (e.g. pra-śam-ya).

[7.1.67] उपसर्गात् खल्घञोः (upasargāt khalghañoḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes the augment num after an upasarga before the kṛt suffixes khal and ghañ.

[7.1.68] न सुदुर्भ्यां केवलाभ्याम् (na sudurbhyāṃ kevalābhyām)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prohibits the num augment after prefixes 'su' and 'dur' when standing alone.

--- 5.2 Prefix-Conditioned Kṛt Affix Derivations (Adhyāya 3) ---

[3.1.100] गदमदचरयमश्चानुपसर्गे (gadamadacarayamaścānupasarge)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes kṛtya gerundive suffix (obligation/fitness) after verbal roots under specified conditions (gadamadacarayamaścānupasarge).

[3.1.136] आतश्चोपसर्गे (ātaścopasarge)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes pūrva-kṛdanta agent suffix (kartari kṛt: ṇvul, tṛc, in, ac, etc.) after verbal roots (ātaścopasarge).

[3.1.138] अनुपसर्गाल्लिम्पविन्दधारिपारिवेद्युदेजिचेतिसातिसाहिभ्यश्च (anupasargāllimpavindadhāripārivedyudejicetisātisāhibhyaśca)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes pūrva-kṛdanta agent suffix (kartari kṛt: ṇvul, tṛc, in, ac, etc.) after verbal roots (anupasargāllimpavindadhāripārivedyudejicetisātisāhibhyaśca).

[3.1.142] दुन्योरनुपसर्गे (dunyoranupasarge)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes pūrva-kṛdanta agent suffix (kartari kṛt: ṇvul, tṛc, in, ac, etc.) after verbal roots (dunyoranupasarge).

[3.2.3] आतोऽनुपसर्गे कः (āto'nupasarge kaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes upapada-kṛt primary nominal suffix conditioned by attendant words or semantic roles (āto'nupasarge kaḥ).

[3.2.61] सत्सूद्विषद्रुहदुहयुजविदभिदच्छिदजिनीराजामुपसर्गेऽपि क्विप् (satsūdviṣadruhaduhayujavidabhidacchidajinīrājāmupasarge'pi kvip)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes upapada-kṛt primary nominal suffix conditioned by attendant words or semantic roles (satsūdviṣadruhaduhayujavidabhidacchidajinīrājāmupasarge'pi kvip).

[3.2.98] पञ्चम्यामजातौ (pañcamyāmajātau)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes upapada-kṛt primary nominal suffix conditioned by attendant words or semantic roles (pañcamyāmajātau).

[3.2.99] उपसर्गे च संज्ञायाम् (upasarge ca saṃjñāyām)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes upapada-kṛt primary nominal suffix conditioned by attendant words or semantic roles (upasarge ca saṃjñāyām).

[3.3.22] उपसर्गे रुवः (upasarge ruvaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes primary nominal suffix in the sense of action (bhāve) or non-agent case roles (akartari ca kārake: ghañ, lyuṭ, ktin, ap, khal) (upasarge ruvaḥ).

[3.3.24] श्रिणीभुवोऽनुपसर्गे (śriṇībhuvo'nupasarge)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes primary nominal suffix in the sense of action (bhāve) or non-agent case roles (akartari ca kārake: ghañ, lyuṭ, ktin, ap, khal) (śriṇībhuvo'nupasarge).

[3.3.29] उन्न्योर्ग्रः (unnyorgraḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes primary nominal suffix in the sense of action (bhāve) or non-agent case roles (akartari ca kārake: ghañ, lyuṭ, ktin, ap, khal) (unnyorgraḥ).

[3.3.43] कर्मव्यतिहारे णच् स्त्रियाम् (karmavyatihāre ṇac striyām)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes primary nominal suffix in the sense of action (bhāve) or non-agent case roles (akartari ca kārake: ghañ, lyuṭ, ktin, ap, khal) (karmavyatihāre ṇac striyām).

[3.3.58] ग्रहवृदृनिश्चिगमश्च (grahavṛdṛniścigamaśca)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes primary nominal suffix in the sense of action (bhāve) or non-agent case roles (akartari ca kārake: ghañ, lyuṭ, ktin, ap, khal) (grahavṛdṛniścigamaśca).

[3.3.59] उपसर्गेऽदः (upasarge'daḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes primary nominal suffix in the sense of action (bhāve) or non-agent case roles (akartari ca kārake: ghañ, lyuṭ, ktin, ap, khal) (upasarge'daḥ).

[3.3.61] व्यधजपोरनुपसर्गे (vyadhajaporanupasarge)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes primary nominal suffix in the sense of action (bhāve) or non-agent case roles (akartari ca kārake: ghañ, lyuṭ, ktin, ap, khal) (vyadhajaporanupasarge).

[3.3.67] मदोऽनुपसर्गे (mado'nupasarge)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes primary nominal suffix in the sense of action (bhāve) or non-agent case roles (akartari ca kārake: ghañ, lyuṭ, ktin, ap, khal) (mado'nupasarge).

[3.3.75] भावेऽनुपसर्गस्य (bhāve'nupasargasya)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes primary nominal suffix in the sense of action (bhāve) or non-agent case roles (akartari ca kārake: ghañ, lyuṭ, ktin, ap, khal) (bhāve'nupasargasya).

[3.3.92] उपसर्गे घोः किः (upasarge ghoḥ kiḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes primary nominal suffix in the sense of action (bhāve) or non-agent case roles (akartari ca kārake: ghañ, lyuṭ, ktin, ap, khal) (upasarge ghoḥ kiḥ).

[3.3.106] आतश्चोपसर्गे (ātaścopasarge)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes primary nominal suffix in the sense of action (bhāve) or non-agent case roles (akartari ca kārake: ghañ, lyuṭ, ktin, ap, khal) (ātaścopasarge).

--- 5.3 Prefix Retroflexion in Kṛtya & Kṛt Affixes (8.4) ---

[8.4.29] कृत्यचः (kṛtyacaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Specific verbal root condition or scope for ṇatva after prefixes (kṛtyacaḥ).

--- 5.4 Prefix Vowel Lengthening before Kṛt Formations (6.3) ---

[6.3.120] शरादीनां च (śarādīnāṃ ca)
   Pāṇinian Role: Lengthens the final vowel of an upasarga or stem in specific kṛdanta compounds (śarādīnāṃ ca).

[6.3.121] इकः वहेऽपीलोः (ikaḥ vahe'pīloḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Lengthens the final vowel of an upasarga before the suffix ghañ when not referring to a human.

[6.3.122] उपसर्गस्य घञ्यमनुष्ये बहुलम् (upasargasya ghañyamanuṣye bahulam)
   Pāṇinian Role: Lengthens the final vowel of an upasarga before ghañ when denoting a limb of the body (svāṅga).

[6.3.123] इकः काशे (ikaḥ kāśe)
   Pāṇinian Role: Lengthens prefix vowels in specific compound formations (ikṣvākudarbhābhyāñca).

[6.3.124] दस्ति (dasti)
   Pāṇinian Role: Optional lengthening of prefix vowels before words vana and gahana.

--- 5.5 Compounding and Accent of Prefix with Kṛdanta (2.2 & 6.2) ---

[2.2.18] कुगतिप्रादयः (kugatiprādayaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes compulsory tatpuruṣa compounding of ku, gati, and prādayaḥ (upasargas) with following eligible nominal words (e.g. pra-bhavati -> prabhavaḥ).

[6.2.139] गतिकारकोपपदात् कृत् (gatikārakopapadāt kṛt)
   Pāṇinian Role: Governs accent retention: a gati/upasarga retains its original accent when preceding a kṛdanta formation (gatikārakopapadāt kṛt).


********************************************************************************
CATEGORY: 6. NĀMADHĀTUS (DENOMINATIVE VERBS: TIṄANTA, KṚDANTA, AND PREFIXES)
********************************************************************************

OVERVIEW:
  This category covers all Pāṇinian rules governing denominative verbs (nāmadhātus) derived from nominal stems (prātipadikas) via suffixes Kyac, Kāmyac, Kyaṅ, Kyaṣ, Ṇiṅ, Ṇic, Kvip, and Yak. It traces their complete grammatical lifecycle: stem formation (3.1), stem vowel lengthening/elision, tiṅanta conjugation (voice allocation, periphrastic perfect with ām, aorist), kṛdanta derivations (participles, gerundives, action nouns), and their interaction with prefixes (including root ṣatva in senaya 8.3.65).

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

--- 6.1 Derivation of Nāmadhātu Stems from Nouns (3.1) ---

[3.1.8] सुप आत्मनः क्यच् (supa ātmanaḥ kyac)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes the denominative suffix 'kyac' (surface 'ya') after an accusative nominal stem to express 'wishing X for oneself' (e.g. putram ātmana icchati -> putrīyati).

[3.1.9] काम्यच्च (kāmyacca)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes the denominative suffix 'kāmyac' (surface 'kāmya') after a nominal stem to express 'desiring X' (e.g. putrakāmyati).

[3.1.10] उपमानादाचारे (upamānādācāre)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes the denominative suffix 'kyac' after a nominal stem denoting an object of comparison in the sense of 'behaving towards X as towards an exemplar' (e.g. kṛṣṇa ivācarati -> kṛṣṇīyati).

[3.1.11] कर्तुः क्यङ् सलोपश्च (kartuḥ kyaṅ salopaśca)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes the denominative suffix 'kyaṅ' (surface 'ya') after a nominative stem to express 'acting like X', with elision of final -s (e.g. śyena ivācarati -> śyenāyate).

[3.1.12] भृशादिभ्यो भुव्यच्वेर्लोपश्च हलः (bhṛśādibhyo bhuvyacverlopaśca halaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes 'kyaṅ' after bhṛśa, etc., in the sense of 'becoming X' (bhū), with elision of stem-final consonant (e.g. bhṛśāyate).

[3.1.13] लोहितादिडाज्भ्यः क्यष् (lohitādiḍājbhyaḥ kyaṣ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes the denominative suffix 'kyaṣ' (surface 'ya', inflecting in both padas) after lohita, etc. (e.g. lohitāyati / lohitāyate).

[3.1.14] कष्टाय क्रमणे (kaṣṭāya kramaṇe)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes 'kyaṅ' after kaṣṭa in the sense of 'striving/advancing towards evil' (kaṣṭāyate).

[3.1.15] कर्मणो रोमन्थतपोभ्यां वर्तिचरोः (karmaṇo romanthatapobhyāṃ varticaroḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes 'kyaṅ' after romantha (chewing cud) and tapas (austerity) in the sense of practicing/performing (e.g. romanthāyate, tapasyati).

[3.1.16] बाष्पोष्मभ्यामुद्वमने (bāṣpoṣmabhyāmudvamane)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes 'kyaṅ' after bāṣpa (tears) and uṣman (steam) in the sense of emitting/producing (e.g. bāṣpāyate, uṣmāyate).

[3.1.17] शब्दवैरकलहाभ्रकण्वमेघेभ्यः करणे (śabdavairakalahābhrakaṇvameghebhyaḥ karaṇe)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes 'kyaṅ' after śabda, vaira, kalaha, abhra, kaṇva, megha in the sense of making/producing (e.g. śabdāyate, vairāyate).

[3.1.18] सुखादिभ्यः कर्तृवेदनायाम् (sukhādibhyaḥ kartṛvedanāyām)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes 'kyaṅ' after sukha, duḥkha, etc., in the sense of experiencing/feeling (e.g. sukhāyate, duḥkhāyate).

[3.1.19] नमोवरिवश्चित्रङः क्यच् (namovarivaścitraṅaḥ kyac)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes 'kyac' after namas, varivas, citra in the sense of performing (e.g. namasyati, varivasyati).

[3.1.20] पुच्छभाण्डचीवराण्णिङ् (pucchabhāṇḍacīvarāṇṇiṅ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes the denominative suffix 'ṇiṅ' (surface 'i/aya', taking ātmanepada) after puccha, bhāṇḍa, cīvara (e.g. utpucchayate, saṃbhaṇḍayate).

[3.1.21] मुण्डमिश्रश्लक्ष्णलवणव्रतवस्त्रहलकलकृततूस्तेभ्यो णिच् (muṇḍamiśraślakṣṇalavaṇavratavastrahalakalakṛtatūstebhyo ṇic)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes the denominative suffix 'ṇic' (surface 'i/aya') after muṇḍa, miśra, ślakṣṇa, lavaṇa, vrata, vastra, hala, kala, kṛta, tūsta in the sense of making/doing (e.g. muṇḍayati, miśrayati).

[3.1.25] सत्यापपाशरूपवीणातूलश्लोकसेनालोमत्वचवर्मवर्णचूर्णचुरादिभ्यो णिच् (satyāpapāśarūpavīṇātūlaślokasenālomatvacavarmavarṇacūrṇacurādibhyo ṇic)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes 'ṇic' after satya, āpa, pāśa, rūpa, vīṇā, tūla, śloka, senā, loma, tvaca, varma, varṇa, cūrṇa and curādi roots (e.g. satyāpayati, saṃsenayati, varṇayati).

[3.1.27] कण्ड्वादिभ्यो यक् (kaṇḍvādibhyo yak)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes the denominative suffix yak after the kaṇḍū class of nominal stems (e.g. kaṇḍūyati / kaṇḍūyate).

[3.1.32] सनाद्यन्ता धातवः (sanādyantā dhātavaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Assigns the designation 'dhātu' (verbal root) to all forms ending in suffixes san, kyac, kāmyac, kyaṅ, kyaṣ, ṇiṅ, ṇic, yak, etc., thereby enabling them to receive verbal and nominal inflection.

[Vārttika on 3.1.11] सर्वप्रातिपदिकेभ्यः क्विब् वक्तव्यः (sarvaprātipadikebhyaḥ kvib vaktavyaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Permits the Kvip suffix after any nominal stem to form a denominative verb (e.g. kṛṣṇa -> kṛṣṇati).

[Vārttika on 3.1.10] सर्वप्रातिपदिकेभ्यः क्विब्वेति वक्तव्यम् (sarvaprātipadikebhyaḥ kvibveti vaktavyam)
   Pāṇinian Role: States that Kvip is optional across all prātipadikas in the sense of behaving like the noun.

--- 6.2 Stem Inflexion & Vocalic Operations in Nāmadhātus (7.1, 7.4, 6.4) ---

[7.1.51] अश्वक्षीरवृषलवणानामात्मप्रीतौ क्यचि (aśvakṣīravṛṣalavaṇānāmātmaprītau kyaci)
   Pāṇinian Role: Lengthens the nominal stem vowel before denominative kyac for aśva, kṣīra, vṛṣa, and lavaṇa when expressing personal pleasure.

[7.4.25] अकृत्सार्वधातुकयोर्दीर्घः (akṛtsārvadhātukayordīrghaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Lengthens the stem-final vowel 'a' to 'ā' before the denominative suffix kyaṅ (e.g. śyena + kyaṅ -> śyenāyate).

[7.4.33] क्यचि च (kyaci ca)
   Pāṇinian Role: Replaces the stem-final short vowel with long 'ī' before the denominative suffix kyac (e.g. putra + kyac -> putrīyati).

[6.4.48] अतो लोपः (ato lopaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes elision (lopa) of stem-final short 'a' before ārdhadhātuka affixes and denominative kyac (e.g. putrīya + Liṭ -> putrīyāñcakāra).

[6.4.49] यस्य हलः (yasya halaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes elision of the denominative suffix 'ya' (of kyac/kyaṅ) when preceded by a consonant before non-vocalic environments (yasya halaḥ).

[6.4.50] क्यस्य विभाषा (kyasya vibhāṣā)
   Pāṇinian Role: Governs optional elision of the denominative kya affix in certain inflected environments.

--- 6.3 Tiṅanta Conjugation of Nāmadhātus ---

[1.3.12] अनुदात्तङित आत्मनेपदम् (anudāttaṅita ātmanepadam)
   Pāṇinian Role: Verbal roots with an anudātta or ṅ as an it-marker take Ātmanepada personal endings in kartari prayoga (e.g. edhate, vartate).

[1.3.78] शेषात् कर्तरि परस्मैपदम् (śeṣāt kartari parasmaipadam)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes Parasmaipada endings for the root when preceded by the specified upasarga or grammatical condition (śeṣāt kartari parasmaipadam).

[3.1.35] कास्प्रत्ययादाममन्त्रे लिटि (kāspratyayādāmamantre liṭi)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes the secondary suffix 'ām' in the perfect tense (Liṭ) after roots beginning with kās or ending in a pratyaya (including denominatives: putrīyāñcakāra).

[3.1.40] कृञ् चानुप्रयुज्यते लिटि (kṛñ cānuprayujyate liṭi)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes that the auxiliary roots kṛ, bhū, or as must be appended after a stem ending in 'ām' in Liṭ (e.g. īkṣāñcakre, putrīyāñcakāra).

[3.1.68] कर्तरि शप् (kartari śap)
   Pāṇinian Role: Inserts the class 1 (bhvādi) vikaraṇa 'śap' (surface 'a') between the verbal stem and a sārvadhātuka suffix in active voice (kartari).

[3.1.43] च्लि लुङि (cli luṅi)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes the general aorist marker 'cli' after verbal roots before personal endings in the aorist tense (Luṅ).

--- 6.4 Kṛdanta Derivations from Nāmadhātus ---

[3.1.91] धातोः (dhātoḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes kṛtya gerundive suffix (obligation/fitness) after verbal roots under specified conditions (dhātoḥ).

[3.2.124] लटः शतृशानचावप्रथमासमानाधिकरणे (laṭaḥ śatṛśānacāvaprathamāsamānādhikaraṇe)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes participial primary nominal suffix (śatṛ, śānac, kvasu, tṛn, etc.) after roots (laṭaḥ śatṛśānacāvaprathamāsamānādhikaraṇe).

[3.1.96] तव्यत्तव्यानीयरः (tavyattavyānīyaraḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes kṛtya gerundive suffix (obligation/fitness) after verbal roots under specified conditions (tavyattavyānīyaraḥ).

[3.3.18] भावे (bhāve)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes primary nominal suffix in the sense of action (bhāve) or non-agent case roles (akartari ca kārake: ghañ, lyuṭ, ktin, ap, khal) (bhāve).

[3.3.114] नपुंसके भावे क्तः (napuṃsake bhāve ktaḥ)
   Pāṇinian Role: Prescribes primary nominal suffix in the sense of action (bhāve) or non-agent case roles (akartari ca kārake: ghañ, lyuṭ, ktin, ap, khal) (napuṃsake bhāve ktaḥ).

--- 6.5 Prefix Interactions with Nāmadhātus ---

[1.4.59] उपसर्गाः क्रियायोगे (upasargāḥ kriyāyoge)
   Pāṇinian Role: Designates the 22 prādi particles as 'upasarga' when connected with a verbal action (kriyāyoga).

[8.3.65] उपसर्गात् सुनोतिसुवतिस्यतिस्तौतिस्तोभतिस्थासेनयसेधसिचसञ्जस्वञ्जाम् (upasargāt sunotisuvatisyatistautistobhatisthāsenayasedhasicasañjasvañjām)
   Pāṇinian Role: Condition for verbal ṣatva (dental s -> retroflex ṣ) after prefixes/augments (upasargāt sunotisuvatisyatistautistobhatisthāsenayasedhasicasañjasvañjām).

[8.4.14] उपसर्गादसमासेऽपि णोपदेशस्य (upasargādasamāse'pi ṇopadeśasya)
   Pāṇinian Role: Specific verbal root condition or scope for ṇatva after prefixes (upasargādasamāse'pi ṇopadeśasya).
